Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in automata theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 611 and the invariant I(A,B) = 129 mod 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to automata theory, we decompose the problem into 611 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 129. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
